## Summary of Current State

### Document: 前端 Turn Playback Session 收口计划

The document is an architectural refactoring plan dated 2026-05-07. Its core objective is to consolidate the scattered "playback turn" state that's currently spread across multiple modules into a single, unified `TurnPlaybackSession` concept.

### Key Problem
The "same turn playback" state is currently scattered across:
- `useAdapterConnection.ts` — `currentTurnId`, `pendingAssistantText*`, `pendingAudio*`, `inboundMotionPlan*`, `audioPlayback*`, `turnFinished*`, `assistantTextDelivery*`
- `usePlaybackCompletionCoordinator.ts` — `playbackCoordination.*` (parallel turn tracking)
- `useModelEngine.ts` — `pendingInboundMotionPayloads` (parallel turn queue)
- `turnPlaybackOrchestratorCore.ts` — `PendingTurnPlaybackGroup` (internal group tracking)

### Current Architecture Map

1. **useAdapterConnection** — The central state holder. Maintains a large reactive state object with all the scattered fields. Handles WebSocket connection, message routing, and stores pending text/audio/motion data. It's the biggest file (~750+ lines).

2. **useTurnPlaybackOrchestrator** — Watches adapter nonce fields and feeds them into the core orchestrator. The core (`turnPlaybackOrchestratorCore.ts`) manages "groups" that represent pending playback turn groups, making decisions about when to release text, audio, and motion together.

3. **usePlaybackCompletionCoordinator** — Has its own parallel `playbackCoordination` reactive state that tracks completion of text/audio/motion for the current turn. Watches adapter nonce fields and motion player status to determine when to send `playback_finished`.

4. **useModelEngine** — Has its own `pendingInboundMotionPayloads` Map keyed by turnId. Manages motion payload queuing with fallback timers. Has its own logic for matching payloads to the current turn.

5. **usePetDesktopController** — The top-level composable that wires everything together. Creates adapter, orchestrator, model engine, completion coordinator, and snapshot publisher. Handles desktop bridge commands.

6. **usePetRuntimeSnapshotPublisher** — Publishes runtime state to the desktop bridge (for auxiliary windows like Settings, Action Lab).

### The Plan's 7 Stages

1. **Stage 1**: Define `TurnPlaybackSession` type, selectors, and store — new files only, no behavior changes
2. **Stage 2**: Adapter dual-writes to session — old fields kept, session gets populated in parallel
3. **Stage 3**: Orchestrator switches to reading from session
4. **Stage 4**: Completion coordinator switches to reading from session
5. **Stage 5**: ModelEngine merges its parallel turn semantics into session
6. **Stage 6**: Delete old scattered fields and compatibility layers
7. **Stage 7**: Update snapshot/debug/docs to expose session

### Key Design Decisions

- Session primary key: `orchestrationId` (with fallback to `turnId` and `ephemeral`)
- Phases: `collecting → ready → playing → settling → completed/failed`
- Audio terminal states: `absent` (no audio for this turn), `idle`, `completed`, `failed`
- Text mode: `replace` (not append) for streaming text
- Interrupt: marks current session as failed, doesn't create new session

### 当前问题：散装轮次状态

同一轮播放的状态散落在 **4 个模块** 的不同字段里，没有统一对象：

| 模块 | 持有的轮次相关字段 |
|---|---|
| `useAdapterConnection` | `currentTurnId`, `currentOrchestrationId`, `pendingAssistantText*`, `pendingAudio*`, `inboundMotionPlan*`, `audioPlaybackStarted*`, `audioPlaybackTerminal*`, `assistantTextDelivery*`, `turnFinished*` |
| `turnPlaybackOrchestratorCore` | `PendingTurnPlaybackGroup`（内部 group，有自己的一套 key 体系） |
| `usePlaybackCompletionCoordinator` | `playbackCoordination.*`（平行的一套完成跟踪状态） |
| `useModelEngine` | `pendingInboundMotionPayloads` Map（独立的 turn 队列，自带 fallback timer） |

模块间通过 **nonce + watch** 间接拼接，每个单点都成立，但组合起来不像一个统一系统。

### 收口计划核心设计

**Session 主键**：`orch:<orchestrationId>` > `turn:<turnId>` > `ephemeral:<serial>`

**Session 状态机**：
```
collecting → ready → playing → settling → completed
         ↘ failed  ↩      ↩ failed      ↩ failed
```

### session.ts

```ts
import type { NormalizedMotionPayload } from "../model-engine/contracts";

export type TurnPlaybackPhase =
  | "collecting"
  | "ready"
  | "playing"
  | "settling"
  | "completed"
  | "failed";

export type AudioTerminalState = "idle" | "completed" | "failed" | "absent";

export type TextReceiveMode = "replace" | "append";

export interface TurnPlaybackSessionText {
  content: string | null;
  receivedAtMs: number | null;
  receiveMode: TextReceiveMode;
  released: boolean;
  delivered: boolean;
}

export interface TurnPlaybackSessionAudio {
  url: string | null;
  receivedAtMs: number | null;
  started: boolean;
  startedAtMs: number | null;
  durationMs: number | null;
  terminal: AudioTerminalState;
  reason: string;
}

export interface TurnPlaybackSessionMotion {
  payload: NormalizedMotionPayload | null;
  receivedAtMs: number | null;
  started: boolean;
  completed: boolean;
}

export interface TurnPlaybackSessionBackend {
  turnStarted: boolean;
  turnFinished: boolean;
  success: boolean | null;
  reason: string;
}

export interface TurnPlaybackSession {
  id: string;
  turnId: string | null;
  interrupted: boolean;
  text: TurnPlaybackSessionText;
  audio: TurnPlaybackSessionAudio;
  motion: TurnPlaybackSessionMotion;
  backend: TurnPlaybackSessionBackend;
  phase: TurnPlaybackPhase;
}

export function createTurnPlaybackSession(
  orchestrationId: string | null,
  turnId: string | null = null,
): TurnPlaybackSession {
  const id = resolveSessionId(orchestrationId, turnId);
  return {
    id,
    turnId,
    interrupted: false,
    text: {
      content: null,
      receivedAtMs: null,
      receiveMode: "replace",
      released: false,
      delivered: false,
    },
    audio: {
      url: null,
      receivedAtMs: null,
      started: false,
      startedAtMs: null,
      durationMs: null,
      terminal: "idle",
      reason: "",
    },
    motion: {
      payload: null,
      receivedAtMs: null,
      started: false,
      completed: false,
    },
    backend: {
      turnStarted: false,
      turnFinished: false,
      success: null,
      reason: "",
    },
    phase: "collecting",
  };
}

function normalizeId(value: unknown): string {
  return typeof value === "string" ? value.trim() : "";
}

export function resolveSessionId(
  orchestrationId: string | null,
  turnId: string | null,
): string {
  const normalizedOrch = normalizeId(orchestrationId);
  if (normalizedOrch) {
    return `orch:${normalizedOrch}`;
  }
  const normalizedTurn = normalizeId(turnId);
  if (normalizedTurn) {
    return `turn:${normalizedTurn}`;
  }
  // ephemeral sessions are created by the store, not here
  return "";
}
```

Let me also think about phase transitions. The document says `markPhase()` must reject illegal transitions. I'll add a validation function.

```ts
const VALID_PHASE_TRANSITIONS: Record<TurnPlaybackPhase, TurnPlaybackPhase[]> = {
  collecting: ["ready", "failed"],
  ready: ["playing", "failed"],
  playing: ["settling", "failed"],
  settling: ["completed", "failed"],
  completed: [],
  failed: [],
};

const TERMINAL_PHASES: Set<TurnPlaybackPhase> = new Set(["completed", "failed"]);

export function isValidPhaseTransition(
  from: TurnPlaybackPhase,
  to: TurnPlaybackPhase,
): boolean {
  if (TERMINAL_PHASES.has(from)) {
    return false;
  }
  return VALID_PHASE_TRANSITIONS[from]?.includes(to) ?? false;
}
```

```ts
export function isSessionReadyForRelease(session: TurnPlaybackSession): boolean {
  return session.text.content !== null && session.phase !== "failed";
}

export function isSessionPlaybackComplete(session: TurnPlaybackSession): boolean {
  return session.phase === "completed";
}
```

### selectors.ts

```ts
import type { TurnPlaybackSession } from "./session";

export function canReleaseText(session: TurnPlaybackSession): boolean {
  return !session.text.released && session.text.content !== null;
}

export function canReleaseAudio(session: TurnPlaybackSession): boolean {
  return session.audio.url !== null && session.audio.terminal === "idle";
}

export function canReleaseMotion(session: TurnPlaybackSession): boolean {
  return !session.motion.started && session.motion.payload !== null;
}

export function shouldWaitForLateMotion(
  session: TurnPlaybackSession,
  nowMs: number,
): boolean {
  // If motion already started or completed, no need to wait
  if (session.motion.started || session.motion.completed) {
    return false;
  }
  // If no motion payload received, don't wait
  if (!session.motion.payload) {
    return false;
  }
  // Motion received but not yet started — should wait
  return true;
}

export function isReadyToAckPlaybackFinished(
  session: TurnPlaybackSession,
): boolean {
  if (!session.text.delivered) {
    return false;
  }
  const audioTerminal = session.audio.terminal;
  if (audioTerminal !== "completed" && audioTerminal !== "failed" && audioTerminal !== "absent") {
    return false;
  }
  if (session.motion.payload !== null && !session.motion.completed) {
    return false;
  }
  if (!session.backend.turnFinished) {
    return false;
  }
  return true;
}
```

Hmm, the `shouldWaitForLateMotion` function from the plan takes `(session, nowMs)`. But in the current code, the late motion decision is timer-based. Let me think about what this selector should do...

Actually, the document says these are "pure functions" that the orchestrator/completion layer reads. The `nowMs` parameter suggests it might involve time calculations. But actually, timing decisions should stay in the orchestrator. The selector should just check if the session state indicates motion is still pending.

Let me keep it simple — `shouldWaitForLateMotion` checks if motion payload exists but hasn't started/completed yet.

### useTurnPlaybackSessionStore.ts

This is the most complex piece. Let me design it carefully.

The store needs to:
1. Hold a Map of sessions
2. Track activeSessionId
3. Provide ensureSession, getSession, setActiveSession
4. Provide all mark* functions
5. Be idempotent
6. Handle phase transitions properly
7. Be a Vue composable (returning reactive state)

Following the existing codebase patterns (like `useAdapterConnection`), I'll use a reactive state object.

```ts
import { reactive, readonly } from "vue";
import {
  createTurnPlaybackSession,
  resolveSessionId,
  isValidPhaseTransition,
  type TurnPlaybackSession,
  type TurnPlaybackPhase,
  type TextReceiveMode,
  type AudioTerminalState,
} from "../turn-playback/session";
import type { NormalizedMotionPayload } from "../model-engine/contracts";

// ...

export function useTurnPlaybackSessionStore() {
  let ephemeralSerial = 0;
  
  const state = reactive({
    sessions: new Map<string, TurnPlaybackSession>(),
    activeSessionId: null as string | null,
  });

    orchestrationId: string | null,
    turnId: string | null = null,
  ): TurnPlaybackSession {
    const resolvedId = resolveSessionId(orchestrationId, turnId);
    const id = resolvedId || nextEphemeralId();
    
    const existing = state.sessions.get(id);
    if (existing) {
      // Update turnId if it was null
      if (!existing.turnId && turnId) {
        existing.turnId = turnId;
      }
      return existing;
    }
    
    const session = createTurnPlaybackSession(orchestrationId, turnId, id);
    state.sessions.set(id, session);
    return session;
  }

    // ...need to resolve and look up
  }
  // ... etc
}
```

### 已创建文件

| 文件 | 行数 | 内容 |
|---|---|---|
| `frontend/src/turn-playback/session.ts` | 186 | 类型定义、phase 状态机、ID 解析、构造函数、基础工具函数 |
| `frontend/src/turn-playback/selectors.ts` | 81 | 5 个纯函数：canReleaseText/Audio/Motion、shouldWaitForLateMotion、isReadyToAckPlaybackFinished |
| `frontend/src/composables/useTurnPlaybackSessionStore.ts` | 377 | Vue reactive store，Map 存储 sessions，17 个 mark 方法 + finalize/prune |

### 关键设计决策

- **Phase 守卫**：`markPhase` 通过 `isValidPhaseTransition` 拒绝非法回退，interrupt 强制进入 `failed`
- **幂等性**：同一事件重复调用不破坏 session（空文本/空 URL 被静默跳过；phase 同名调用直接返回 true）
- **text.released 语义**：`markTextDelivered` 自动置 `released = true`（交付隐含释放）
- **Store 被动**：不自动推进 phase，由上层（orchestrator/completion）显式调用 `markPhase`

The log shows "illegal phase transition: collecting -> completed" — because in the test `testStoreIllegalPhaseTransitionRejected`, I call `store.markPhase("orch-1", "turn-1", "completed")` which tries to go directly from `collecting` to `completed`, skipping `ready`, `playing`, `settling`. The valid transitions are:
   - collecting → ready (not completed)
   - collecting → failed

So `markPhase("orch-1", "turn-1", "completed")` returns `false` (transition rejected). The test then checks `assert.equal(store.markPhase("orch-1", "turn-1", "playing"), false)` — but the phase is still `collecting`, not `completed`. So the second assertion should be against `collecting`, not `completed`.

Let me fix the test:

```ts
function testStoreIllegalPhaseTransitionRejected(): void {
  const store = useTurnPlaybackSessionStore();

  // Try to skip directly to completed — should be rejected
  assert.equal(store.markPhase("orch-1", "turn-1", "completed"), false);
  const s = store.getSession("orch-1")!;
  assert.equal(s.phase, "collecting"); // still in collecting
  
  // Now do valid transitions
  assert.equal(store.markPhase("orch-1", "turn-1", "ready"), true);
  assert.equal(store.markPhase("orch-1", "turn-1", "playing"), true);
  assert.equal(store.markPhase("orch-1", "turn-1", "settling"), true);
  assert.equal(store.markPhase("orch-1", "turn-1", "completed"), true);
  
  // Cannot leave terminal state
  assert.equal(store.markPhase("orch-1", "turn-1", "playing"), false);
  assert.equal(s.phase, "completed");
}
```

```
  ephemeral: false
}
```

The session ID is `turn:turn-ephemeral`, not `ephemeral:...`. That's because `resolveSessionId(null, "turn-ephemeral")` returns `"turn:turn-ephemeral"` — it falls back to the `turn:` prefix since the turnId is present. The ephemeral fallback only triggers when BOTH orchestrationId AND turnId are null/empty.

So `sessions[0].id.startsWith("ephemeral:")` returns `false`.

The test expectation is wrong. The session ID should be `"turn:turn-ephemeral"` since we did provide a turnId. The ephemeral prefix only kicks in when both IDs are missing.

```ts
  turnId: string | null,
  orchestrationId: string | null,

): void {

}
```

Let me design the new orchestrator:

```ts
export function useTurnPlaybackOrchestrator(options: {
  sessionStore: ReturnType<typeof useTurnPlaybackSessionStore>;
  modelEngine: ModelEngine;
  adapter: AdapterConnection; // still needed for releaseText/releaseAudio side effects
}) {
  const core = createTurnPlaybackOrchestratorCore({
    now: () => performance.now(),
    schedule: (delayMs, fn) => window.setTimeout(fn, delayMs),
    clearSchedule: (timer) => window.clearTimeout(timer as number),
    releaseText: (turnId, orchestrationId) => {
      // Trigger existing text release (for UI display)
      const released = options.adapter.releaseAssistantTextForPlayback(turnId, orchestrationId);
      // Write to session
      options.sessionStore.markTextReleased(orchestrationId, turnId);
      return released;
    },
    releaseAudio: (turnId, orchestrationId) => {
      void options.adapter.releaseAudioForPlayback(turnId, orchestrationId);
      // Audio started will be written by audioPlaybackActions (Stage 2)
    },
    releaseMotion: (payload, context) => {
      options.modelEngine.ingestInboundPayload(payload, context);
      options.sessionStore.markMotionStarted(context.orchestrationId, context.turnId);
    },
    log: (message, details) => {
      console.info(`[TurnPlaybackOrchestrator] ${message}.`, details);
    },
  });

  // Watch active session changes
  watch(
    () => options.sessionStore.state.activeSessionId,
    (sessionId) => {
      if (!sessionId) return;
      core.flush(); // clear stale groups
    },
  );

  // Watch session state and feed to core
  // ... need to figure out how to watch nested reactive properties
}
```

```ts
function ingestInboundPayload(payload: unknown, context: InboundPayloadContext): void {

}
```

For the orchestrator rewrite, here's my design:

```ts
import { onScopeDispose, watch } from "vue";
import type { useModelEngine } from "../model-engine/useModelEngine";
import type { useTurnPlaybackSessionStore } from "./useTurnPlaybackSessionStore";
import type { useAdapterConnection } from "./useAdapterConnection";
import {
  createTurnPlaybackOrchestratorCore,
  type TurnPlaybackReleaseContext,
} from "./turnPlaybackOrchestratorCore";
import { orchestrationIdFromSessionId } from "../turn-playback/session";

type AdapterConnection = ReturnType<typeof useAdapterConnection>;
type ModelEngine = ReturnType<typeof useModelEngine>;
type SessionStore = ReturnType<typeof useTurnPlaybackSessionStore>;

export function useTurnPlaybackOrchestrator(options: {
  sessionStore: SessionStore;
  adapter: AdapterConnection;
  modelEngine: ModelEngine;
}) {
  const core = createTurnPlaybackOrchestratorCore({
    now: () => performance.now(),
    schedule: (delayMs, fn) => window.setTimeout(fn, delayMs),
    clearSchedule: (timer) => window.clearTimeout(timer as number),
    releaseText: (turnId, orchestrationId) => {
      const released = options.adapter.releaseAssistantTextForPlayback(turnId, orchestrationId);
      if (released) {
        options.sessionStore.markTextReleased(orchestrationId, turnId);
      }
      return released;
    },
    releaseAudio: (turnId, orchestrationId) => {
      void options.adapter.releaseAudioForPlayback(turnId, orchestrationId);
    },
    releaseMotion: (payload: unknown, context: TurnPlaybackReleaseContext) => {
      options.modelEngine.ingestNormalizedPayload(payload as NormalizedMotionPayload, context);
      options.sessionStore.markMotionStarted(context.orchestrationId, context.turnId);
      options.sessionStore.markPhase(context.orchestrationId, context.turnId, "playing");
    },
    log: (message, details) => {
      console.info(`[TurnPlaybackOrchestrator] ${message}.`, details);
    },
  });

  // Watch session text content → mark text ready
  let lastTextNonce = 0;
  watch(
    () => {
      const s = options.sessionStore.getActiveSession();
      return s ? `${s.id}|${s.text.content ?? ""}` : null;
    },
    (key) => {
      if (!key) return;
      const s = options.sessionStore.getActiveSession();
      if (!s || !s.text.content || s.text.released) return;
      lastTextNonce++;
      core.markTextReady(s.turnId, orchestrationIdFromSessionId(s.id));
    },
  );

  // Watch session audio url → mark audio ready
  watch(
    () => {
      const s = options.sessionStore.getActiveSession();
      return s?.audio.url ?? null;
    },
    (url) => {
      if (!url) return;
      const s = options.sessionStore.getActiveSession();
      if (!s) return;
      core.markAudioReady(s.turnId, orchestrationIdFromSessionId(s.id));
    },
  );

  // Watch session audio terminal → mark no audio
  watch(
    () => {
      const s = options.sessionStore.getActiveSession();
      return s ? `${s.id}|${s.audio.terminal}` : null;
    },
    (key) => {
      if (!key) return;
      const s = options.sessionStore.getActiveSession();
      if (!s) return;
      if (s.audio.terminal === "absent") {
        core.markNoAudioConfirmed(s.turnId, orchestrationIdFromSessionId(s.id));
      }
    },
  );

  // Watch session motion payload → mark motion ready
  watch(
    () => {
      const s = options.sessionStore.getActiveSession();
      return s?.motion.payload ?? null;
    },
    (payload) => {
      if (!payload) return;
      const s = options.sessionStore.getActiveSession();
      if (!s || s.motion.started) return;
      core.markMotionReady(
        s.turnId,
        orchestrationIdFromSessionId(s.id),
        payload,
        s.motion.receivedAtMs ?? performance.now(),
      );
    },
  );

  // Watch session backend.turnFinished → mark turn finished
  watch(
    () => {
      const s = options.sessionStore.getActiveSession();
      return s ? `${s.id}|${s.backend.turnFinished}` : null;
    },
    (key) => {
      if (!key) return;
      const s = options.sessionStore.getActiveSession();
      if (!s || !s.backend.turnFinished) return;
      core.markTurnFinished(s.turnId, orchestrationIdFromSessionId(s.id));
      
      // Update session phase to "ready" if text is present
      if (s.text.content && s.phase === "collecting") {
        options.sessionStore.markPhase(
          orchestrationIdFromSessionId(s.id),
          s.turnId,
          "ready",
        );
      }
    },
  );

  // When active session changes, flush the core for stale groups
  watch(
    () => options.sessionStore.state.activeSessionId,
    () => {
      core.flush();
    },
  );

  // Forward turn changes to model engine
  watch(
    () => options.sessionStore.state.activeSessionId,
    (sessionId) => {
      const s = sessionId ? options.sessionStore.state.sessions.get(sessionId) : undefined;
      options.modelEngine.notifyCurrentTurnChanged(s?.turnId ?? null);
    },
  );

  onScopeDispose(() => {
    core.clear();
  });

  return {
    flush: () => core.flush(),
  };
}
```

```ts
export function usePlaybackCompletionCoordinator(options: {
  sessionStore: SessionStore;
  adapter: AdapterConnection;
  motionPlayer: PreviewMotionPlayer;
  selectedModel: Ref<ModelSummary | null>;
  onAudioPlaybackStarted: (turnId: string | null) => void;
  initialMotionPlaybackRecords?: readonly DesktopMotionPlaybackRecord[];
  maxMotionPlaybackRecords?: number;
}) {
  // motion playback records (unchanged)
  const motionPlaybackRecords = ref<DesktopMotionPlaybackRecord[]>(...);
  const maxMotionPlaybackRecords = ...;
  
  // Lightweight internal state
  let completionSent = false;
  let settlementTimer = 0;
  let awaitingLateMotion = false;
  let activeOrchId: string | null = null;
  let activeTurnId: string | null = null;

  function clearSettlementTimer() { ... }
  function resetInternal() {
    clearSettlementTimer();
    completionSent = false;
    awaitingLateMotion = false;
    activeOrchId = null;
    activeTurnId = null;
  }
  
  function getActiveSession() {
    return options.sessionStore.getActiveSession();
  }

  function isPlaybackAckRequired(): boolean {
    const s = getActiveSession();
    if (!s) return false;
    return s.audio.terminal !== "idle";
  }

  function finalizeCompletion(): void {
    const s = getActiveSession();
    if (!s) return;
    const orchId = orchestrationIdFromSessionId(s.id);
    options.adapter.clearPlaybackGroupContext(s.turnId, orchId);
    options.sessionStore.markPhase(orchId, s.turnId, "completed");
    resetInternal();
  }

  function maybeFlushPlaybackCompletion(reason?: string): void {
    if (completionSent) {
      const s = getActiveSession();
      if (s?.backend.turnFinished) {
        finalizeCompletion();
      }
      return;
    }
    const s = getActiveSession();
    if (!s) return;
    if (!isReadyToAckPlaybackFinished(s)) return;
    if (!isPlaybackAckRequired()) {
      if (s.backend.turnFinished) {
        finalizeCompletion();
      }
      return;
    }
    const orchId = orchestrationIdFromSessionId(s.id);
    const success = s.audio.terminal !== "failed";
    completionSent = true;
    options.sessionStore.markPhase(orchId, s.turnId, "settling");
    void options.adapter.sendPlaybackFinishedForCurrentGroup(
      s.turnId, orchId, success, reason,
    );
  }

  function schedulePlaybackSettlementWindow(): void {
    clearSettlementTimer();
    awaitingLateMotion = true;
    settlementTimer = window.setTimeout(() => {
      awaitingLateMotion = false;
      const s = getActiveSession();
      if (s && !s.motion.started) {
        // Motion never started — consider it not expected
        // (The session's motion payload doesn't have an "expected" flag,
        // but the coordinator can track this internally)
      }
      maybeFlushPlaybackCompletion("audio_and_motion_settled");
    }, PLAYBACK_SETTLEMENT_WINDOW_MS);
  }

  function recordMotionPlayback(event: ModelEnginePlanStartedEvent): void {
    // Update tracking
    activeOrchId = event.orchestrationId;
    activeTurnId = event.turnId;
    awaitingLateMotion = false;
    clearSettlementTimer();
    
    // Write to session
    options.sessionStore.markMotionStarted(event.orchestrationId, event.turnId);
    
    // Create record (unchanged)
    ...
  }

  // ── Watchers ──

  // Active session changed → reset internal state
  watch(
    () => options.sessionStore.state.activeSessionId,
    () => resetInternal(),
  );

  // Audio started
  watch(
    () => {
      const s = getActiveSession();
      return s?.audio.started ? `${s.id}|started` : null;
    },
    (key) => {
      if (!key) return;
      const s = getActiveSession();
      if (!s) return;
      options.onAudioPlaybackStarted(s.turnId);
      maybeFlushPlaybackCompletion("audio_started");
    },
  );

  // Text delivered
  watch(
    () => {
      const s = getActiveSession();
      return s?.text.delivered ? `${s.id}|delivered` : null;
    },
    (key) => {
      if (!key) return;
      maybeFlushPlaybackCompletion("text_delivered");
    },
  );

  // Audio terminal
  watch(
    () => {
      const s = getActiveSession();
      if (!s || s.audio.terminal === "idle") return null;
      return `${s.id}|${s.audio.terminal}`;
    },
    (key) => {
      if (!key) return;
      const s = getActiveSession();
      if (!s) return;
      if (!s.motion.started || s.motion.completed) {
        schedulePlaybackSettlementWindow();
        return;
      }
      maybeFlushPlaybackCompletion(s.audio.reason || "audio_terminal");
    },
  );

  // Motion completed (from player)
  watch(
    () => options.motionPlayer.state.status,
    (status, prev) => {
      if (status !== "finished" || prev !== "playing") return;
      const s = getActiveSession();
      if (!s) return;
      options.sessionStore.markMotionCompleted(
        orchestrationIdFromSessionId(s.id), s.turnId,
      );
      if (s.audio.terminal !== "idle") {
        maybeFlushPlaybackCompletion("motion_completed_after_audio");
      }
    },
  );

  // Backend turn finished
  watch(
    () => {
      const s = getActiveSession();
      return s?.backend.turnFinished ? `${s.id}|finished` : null;
    },
    (key) => {
      if (!key) return;
      maybeFlushPlaybackCompletion("turn_finished");
    },
  );

  return { motionPlaybackRecords, recordMotionPlayback, resetPlaybackCoordination: resetInternal };
}
```

1. When the active session changes mid-completion: the `activeSessionId` watcher resets internal state, so stale completion won't fire.
2. When text is delivered before audio: the text watcher fires, `maybeFlushPlaybackCompletion` checks `isReadyToAckPlaybackFinished` which requires audio terminal — so it won't prematurely ack.
3. When motion finishes after audio: the settlement window handles this.
4. Double-send prevention: `completionSent` flag.

```ts
function queueInboundPayload(payload, context): void {
  const normalizedTurnId = normalizeTurnId(context.turnId);
  if (!normalizedTurnId) {
    startPayload(payload, { turnId: null, ... });
    return;
  }

  // Replace existing pending for same turn
  const existing = pendingInboundMotionPayloads.get(normalizedTurnId);
  if (existing) { clearPendingPayload(existing); pendingInboundMotionPayloads.delete(normalizedTurnId); }

  const entry = { payload, turnId: normalizedTurnId, ... };
  
  // Fallback timer
  entry.fallbackTimer = window.setTimeout(() => {
    const latest = pendingInboundMotionPayloads.get(normalizedTurnId);
    if (!latest || latest !== entry) return;
    
    const currentTurnId = normalizeTurnId(dependencies.getCurrentTurnId());
    const currentOrchestrationId = dependencies.getCurrentOrchestrationId?.() ?? null;
    const audioOrchestrationId = dependencies.getAudioPlaybackInfo().orchestrationId ?? null;
    
    if (currentTurnId && currentTurnId !== normalizedTurnId) {
      if (entry.orchestrationId && (entry.orchestrationId === currentOrchestrationId || entry.orchestrationId === audioOrchestrationId)) {
        tryStartPendingPayload(normalizedTurnId, "wait_audio_timeout_orchestration_match");
        return;
      }
      clearPendingPayload(entry);
      pendingInboundMotionPayloads.delete(normalizedTurnId);
      syncPendingState();
      return;
    }
    
    tryStartPendingPayload(normalizedTurnId, "wait_audio_timeout");
  }, MOTION_SYNC_WAIT_FOR_AUDIO_MS);

  pendingInboundMotionPayloads.set(normalizedTurnId, entry);

  // Check if audio already playing
  const activeAudioTurnId = normalizeTurnId(dependencies.getAudioPlaybackInfo().turnId);
  const activeAudioOrchestrationId = dependencies.getAudioPlaybackInfo().orchestrationId ?? null;
  if ((activeAudioTurnId && activeAudioTurnId === normalizedTurnId) || (entry.orchestrationId && activeAudioOrchestrationId === entry.orchestrationId)) {
    tryStartPendingPayload(normalizedTurnId, "audio_already_playing");
  }
}
```

## Review Findings

### 1. **Session Model & Store (session.ts, selectors.ts, useTurnPlaybackSessionStore.ts)** — ✅ Good

- Phase machine is clean: `collecting → ready → playing → settling → completed/failed`
- ID resolution follows the spec: `orch: > turn: > ephemeral:`
- Store is idempotent (empty strings skipped, duplicate calls handled)
- `markTextDelivered` correctly sets `released = true` (delivery implies release per spec)
- `markInterrupt` correctly marks the active session as failed

**Minor issue**: `isSessionPlaybackComplete` checks `phase === "completed"` which is correct. `isSessionReadyForRelease` also exists but is only used in tests. This is fine per the plan.

### 2. **Adapter Dual-Writes** — ✅ Good

- `control.turn_started`: sets active session + marks turn started
- `output.text`: marks text received in session
- `output.audio`: marks text + audio received
- `control.turn_finished`: marks turn finished
- `control.interrupt`: marks interrupt
- `engine.motion_*`: normalizes and marks motion received
- `markAudioPlaybackTerminal`: dual-writes to session (maps `not_requested → absent`)
- `releaseAssistantTextForPlayback`: marks text delivered
- `audioPlaybackActions.ts`: marks audio started in session

### 3. **Orchestrator** — ⚠️ One concern

The orchestrator watches session state changes via composite key strings (e.g., `${s.id}|${s.text.content}`). This works but has a subtle issue: 

In the **text watcher**, the composite key includes `s.text.released`. When text is released, the key changes from `"orch:1|hello|false"` to `"orch:1|hello|true"`. But the watcher callback checks `if (s.text.released) return;` — so it won't re-mark text as ready. Good.

However, there's a potential issue: if text content changes from "hello" to "hello world" (replace mode, second text message), the key changes from `"orch:1|hello|false"` to `"orch:1|hello world|false"`. But the core's `markTextReady` has already been called for this group. Looking at the core:

```ts
markTextReady: (turnId, orchestrationId) => {
  const group = getOrCreateGroup(turnId, orchestrationId);
  group.textReady = true;
  evaluateGroup(group, "text_ready");
},
```

If `textReady` is already true, it just calls `evaluateGroup` again, which is idempotent. So this is fine.

### 4. **Completion Coordinator** — ⚠️ Two issues

**Issue A**: In the audio terminal watcher:

```ts
if (!s.motion.started || s.motion.completed) {
  schedulePlaybackSettlementWindow();
  return;
}
maybeFlushPlaybackCompletion(s.audio.reason || "audio_terminal");
```

When `motion.started` is false (motion never started) AND audio is terminal, it schedules a settlement window. After the 900ms window, `maybeFlushPlaybackCompletion` is called. But `isReadyToAckPlaybackFinished` checks `motion.payload !== null && !motion.completed`. If there's no motion payload at all, this condition passes. If there IS a payload but motion never started, `motion.completed` is false, so `isReadyToAckPlaybackFinished` returns false.

The settlement window was supposed to handle this: after 900ms, if motion still hasn't started, it should consider motion "not expected". But in the new code, the settlement callback doesn't set any flag — it just calls `maybeFlushPlaybackCompletion`. 

In the old code, the settlement callback did:
```ts
if (!playbackCoordination.motionStarted) {
  playbackCoordination.motionExpected = false;
}
```

This `motionExpected` flag was checked in `shouldSendPlaybackFinished`:
```ts
if (!playbackCoordination.motionExpected) return true; // motion not expected, ok to ack
```

But in the new code, there's no equivalent logic. `isReadyToAckPlaybackFinished` doesn't have a "motionExpected" concept. It just checks `motion.payload !== null && !motion.completed`.

**This means**: If a motion payload exists but never starts (e.g., audio finished before motion could start), the settlement window fires but `isReadyToAckPlaybackFinished` still returns false because `motion.payload !== null && !motion.completed`. The completion ack will never be sent.

**Fix needed**: In the settlement window callback, if motion never started and we're past the settlement window, I should write `sessionStore.markMotionCompleted(...)` (or treat it as "not applicable"). Or, add a "motion expected" concept back.

Actually, looking at the current flow more carefully:
- The orchestrator's `releaseMotion` callback calls `markMotionStarted` and `markPhase("playing")`. If the motion payload exists but the orchestrator hasn't released it yet (still in timing window), `motion.started` is false.
- When audio terminal arrives, the coordinator sees `motion.started === false` and schedules a settlement window.
- During the 900ms, if the orchestrator releases motion, `motion.started` becomes true via `recordMotionPlayback`.
- After 900ms, if motion still hasn't started, it means the orchestrator decided not to wait for motion (or motion was late and the orchestrator's group was already released).

The problem is that when the orchestrator decides NOT to release motion (because the audio-motion sync window expired), it calls `releaseGroup` which releases text and audio but doesn't release motion. In that case, `motion.payload` is still non-null and `motion.started` is still false.

The fix: in the settlement window callback, if motion hasn't started after the window, mark it as completed (effectively skipping it):

```ts
settlementTimer = window.setTimeout(() => {
  awaitingLateMotion = false;
  const s = getActiveSession();
  if (s && s.motion.payload && !s.motion.started) {
    options.sessionStore.markMotionCompleted(
      orchestrationIdFromSessionId(s.id),
      s.turnId,
    );
  }
  maybeFlushPlaybackCompletion("audio_and_motion_settled");
}, PLAYBACK_SETTLEMENT_WINDOW_MS);
```

This way, after the settlement window, motion is considered "done" (skipped), and `isReadyToAckPlaybackFinished` will pass.

**Issue B**: The `awaitingLateMotion` variable is set but never checked. In the old code, it prevented `shouldSendPlaybackFinished` from returning true. In the new code, `isReadyToAckPlaybackFinished` doesn't check it. But since `awaitingLateMotion` is set when scheduling the settlement window, and the settlement window's timer callback clears it before calling `maybeFlushPlaybackCompletion`, the race condition is handled by the timer. So this is actually fine — `awaitingLateMotion` is a guard against premature flushing from OTHER watchers during the settlement window, but those other watchers call `maybeFlushPlaybackCompletion` which uses `isReadyToAckPlaybackFinished`, which checks the actual session state. Since the session state hasn't changed during the settlement window, the completion check will still fail. So `awaitingLateMotion` being unused is harmless but dead code.

Actually wait, `awaitingLateMotion` IS set but the new code never reads it. In the old code:
```ts
function shouldSendPlaybackFinished(): boolean {
  ...
  if (playbackCoordination.awaitingLateMotion) return false;
  ...
}
```

In the new code, there's no equivalent check. But `isReadyToAckPlaybackFinished` reads from session state, which doesn't change during the settlement window. So the check isn't needed — the session-based approach naturally handles this. `awaitingLateMotion` is dead code and should be removed.

### 5. **Model Engine** — ✅ Good

- `resolveMotionTargetDurationMs` prefers session, falls back to dependency callback
- `queueInboundPayload`'s fallback timer reads from session
- `ingestNormalizedPayload` added cleanly
- Backward compatible via optional `sessionStore`

### 6. **Snapshot Publisher** — ✅ Good

- Session fields exposed in snapshot
- Session state changes trigger snapshot republish via the watcher array

### Summary of Issues Found:

1. **Critical**: Settlement window doesn't handle case where motion never starts — needs to mark motion as completed/skipped after the window expires
2. **Minor**: `awaitingLateMotion` variable is dead code (set but never read)

Let me fix both issues.

## Section 5: Session Model

### 5.1 Session 主键
- Plan: `orch:<orchestrationId>` > `turn:<turnId>` > `ephemeral:<serial>`
- Implementation: ✅ `resolveSessionId` follows this exactly. `ensureSession` in the store adds ephemeral suffix when both IDs are missing.
- Constraint: "一旦 session 以某种 id 创建，当前轮次内保持不变" — ✅ ensured by `ensureSession` checking existing sessions first.

### 5.2 建议类型
- Plan specifies `TurnPlaybackSession` interface with specific sub-interfaces
- Implementation check:
  - `TurnPlaybackPhase`: ✅ "collecting" | "ready" | "playing" | "settling" | "completed" | "failed"
  - `id: string`: ✅
  - `turnId: string | null`: ✅
  - `interrupted: boolean`: ✅
  - `text`: ✅ all fields match (content, receivedAtMs, receiveMode, released, delivered)
  - `audio`: ✅ all fields match (url, receivedAtMs, started, startedAtMs, durationMs, terminal, reason)
  - `motion`: ✅ all fields match (payload, receivedAtMs, started, completed)
  - `backend`: ✅ all fields match (turnStarted, turnFinished, success, reason)
  - `phase: TurnPlaybackPhase`: ✅

Wait, the plan shows `receiveMode: "replace" | "append"` as a type with `"replace" | "append"`, but the implementation defines `TextReceiveMode` type separately. ✅ functionally equivalent.

### 5.3 字段语义要求
- `audio.terminal = "absent"` semantics: ✅ mapped from old `not_requested` in `markAudioPlaybackTerminal`
- `backend.turnFinished` doesn't mean frontend completion: ✅ `isReadyToAckPlaybackFinished` requires all 4 conditions, not just `turnFinished`
- `interrupted = true` distinct from normal failure: ✅ `markInterrupt` sets `interrupted = true` + `phase = "failed"` + `backend.reason = "interrupted"`

### 5.4 流式文本累积规则
- Default `replace` mode: ✅ `markTextReceived` defaults to `"replace"`
- Multiple `output.text` replaces: ✅ `markTextReceived` overwrites content in replace mode

### 5.5 中断规则
1. interrupt doesn't create new session: ✅ `markInterrupt` uses `getActiveSession() ?? ensureSession(...)` — falls back to ensureSession only when no active session (edge case), but never creates a NEW session for the interrupt
2. Marks `interrupted = true`, `backend.reason = "interrupted"`, `phase = "failed"`: ✅
3. New session only on new orchestrationId: ✅ `setActiveSession` in `control.turn_started` handler

Actually wait, there's a subtle issue with the interrupt rule. The plan says "interrupt 不负责创建新 session" and "当前 active session 在收到 interrupt 时" should be marked. But the implementation's `markInterrupt` falls back to `ensureSession` if no active session exists. This fallback COULD create a new session if neither exists. But this is an edge case — if there's no active session, what are we interrupting? The fallback is defensive. I should check if this edge case could actually happen in practice.

In `handleSocketMessage`, the `control.interrupt` case:
```ts
case "control.interrupt":
  stopAudioPlayback();
  resetAudioPlaybackTerminal();
  sessionStore?.markInterrupt(state.currentOrchestrationId, state.currentTurnId);
  state.currentTurnId = null;
  state.currentOrchestrationId = null;
```

Here, `state.currentOrchestrationId` could be null if no turn has started. If it's null, `getActiveSession()` returns undefined, and `ensureSession(null, null)` creates an ephemeral session and marks it as interrupted. This seems like it could create a spurious interrupted session.

However, in practice, `control.interrupt` should only arrive when there's an active turn. And even if it creates a spurious ephemeral session, it'll be pruned later. Low impact.

### 5.6 Phase 合法迁移
- Main path: `collecting -> ready -> playing -> settling -> completed`: ✅
- Failure paths: all four phases can go to `failed`: ✅
- Completed/failed are terminal: ✅ `isValidPhaseTransition` returns false for terminal phases
- `interrupt` goes to `failed`: ✅ via `markInterrupt` → `markPhaseInternal(session, "failed")`
- `markPhase` rejects illegal transitions: ✅ `isValidPhaseTransition` check

### 5. Session 模型 ✅ 完全吻合

| 规格项 | 实现 | 状态 |
|---|---|---|
| `orch: > turn: > ephemeral:` ID 优先级 | `resolveSessionId` 精确实现 | ✅ |
| session 一旦创建 ID 不可变 | `ensureSession` 先查 Map 再创建 | ✅ |
| `TurnPlaybackPhase` 六态 | `collecting/ready/playing/settling/completed/failed` | ✅ |
| `audio.terminal = "absent"` 语义 | `not_requested → absent` 映射在 `markAudioPlaybackTerminal` | ✅ |
| `interrupted` 显式区分普通失败 | `markInterrupt` 设 `interrupted=true` + `phase=failed` + `reason="interrupted"` | ✅ |
| text 默认 `replace` 模式 | `markTextReceived` 默认 `"replace"` | ✅ |
| Phase 终态不可回退 | `isValidPhaseTransition` 拒绝 `completed/failed` 为起点的迁移 | ✅ |
| 中断不进新 session | `markInterrupt` 用 `getActiveSession()` | ⚠️ 见下文 |

### ⚠️ 发现一个边界问题

`control.interrupt` handler 传 `state.currentOrchestrationId` 给 `markInterrupt`。如果收到 interrupt 时还没有 active session（极端情况），`markInterrupt` 的 fallback `ensureSession(null, null)` 会创建一个 ephemeral session 再标记中断——**违反了"interrupt 不创建新 session"的约束**。

**修复**：在 `markInterrupt` 开头加 guard——无 active session 时直接 return。

```ts
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { ref, nextTick } from "vue";
import { useTurnPlaybackSessionStore } from "../src/composables/useTurnPlaybackSessionStore.js";
import { usePlaybackCompletionCoordinator } from "../src/composables/usePlaybackCompletionCoordinator.js";

// Helper to wait for Vue watchers
function flushWatchers(): Promise<void> {
  return nextTick();
}

function createTestHarness() {
  const sessionStore = useTurnPlaybackSessionStore();
  const playbackFinishedCalls: Array<{...}> = [];
  const clearContextCalls: Array<{...}> = [];
  
  const mockAdapter = {
    state: { lastAssistantText: "" },
    sendPlaybackFinishedForCurrentGroup(turnId, orchId, success, reason) {
      playbackFinishedCalls.push({ turnId, orchId, success, reason });
      return Promise.resolve();
    },
    clearPlaybackGroupContext(turnId, orchId) {
      clearContextCalls.push({ turnId, orchId });
    },
  };
  
  const mockMotionPlayer = {
    state: { status: "idle" as string },
  };
  
  const selectedModel = ref(null);
  
  let audioStartedTurnId: string | null = null;
  
  const coordinator = usePlaybackCompletionCoordinator({
    sessionStore,
    adapter: mockAdapter as any,
    motionPlayer: mockMotionPlayer as any,
    selectedModel,
    onAudioPlaybackStarted: (turnId) => {
      audioStartedTurnId = turnId;
    },
    initialMotionPlaybackRecords: [],
  });
  
  return {
    sessionStore,
    coordinator,
    mockMotionPlayer,
    playbackFinishedCalls,
    clearContextCalls,
    audioStartedTurnId,
    flush: () => nextTick(),
  };
}

// Test: Full completion flow
async function testFullCompletion() {
  const h = createTestHarness();
  
  // Set up a session
  h.sessionStore.setActiveSession("orch-1");
  const s = h.sessionStore.ensureSession("orch-1", "turn-1");
  
  // Text delivered
  s.text.content = "Hello";
  s.text.delivered = true;
  s.text.released = true;
  await h.flush();
  
  // Audio started and completed
  s.audio.url = "http://x/audio.wav";
  s.audio.started = true;
  s.audio.startedAtMs = 100;
  s.audio.durationMs = 5000;
  await h.flush();
  
  s.audio.terminal = "completed";
  await h.flush();
  
  // Motion completed
  s.motion.payload = { kind: "semantic_intent" } as any;
  s.motion.started = true;
  s.motion.completed = true;
  await h.flush();
  
  // Backend turn finished
  s.backend.turnFinished = true;
  s.backend.success = true;
  await h.flush();
  
  // Check that playback_finished was sent
  assert.equal(h.playbackFinishedCalls.length, 1);
  assert.equal(h.playbackFinishedCalls[0].success, true);
}
```

Wait, there's a problem. The watchers fire when the session's reactive properties change. But since the session's phase is now "completed", `isReadyToAckPlaybackFinished` should return false (because... wait, does it check phase? No, it checks text.delivered, audio.terminal, motion.completed, backend.turnFinished). So if the session still has all conditions met, `isReadyToAckPlaybackFinished` would return true again. But looking at the flow:
1. `maybeFlushPlaybackCompletion` is called
2. `completionSent` is false → enters the main path
3. Checks pass → `completionSent = true`, sends ack, marks settling
4. `s.backend.turnFinished` is true → calls `finalizeCompletion()`
5. `finalizeCompletion` marks completed, clears context, calls `resetPlaybackCoordination()` which sets `completionSent = false`
6. Returns to `maybeFlushPlaybackCompletion` → returns to watcher
7. No more watcher fires (nothing changed)

This is fine. `completionSent` being reset to false is OK because the session is now in `completed` phase and the context is cleared.

But wait — `finalizeCompletion` is also called from `maybeFlushPlaybackCompletion`'s early return path:
```ts
if (completionSent) {
  const s = getActiveSession();
  if (s?.backend.turnFinished) {
    finalizeCompletion();
  }
  return;
}
```

If `maybeFlushPlaybackCompletion` is called again (from another watcher after the first call), it would enter this path, but `completionSent` was reset to false by `resetPlaybackCoordination`. So it wouldn't enter the `completionSent` path. It would enter the main path again and potentially send a duplicate ack!

This IS a problem. After `finalizeCompletion` resets `completionSent`, if another watcher fires, it could trigger another ack.

To fix this, I should not reset `completionSent` in `resetPlaybackCoordination` when it's called from `finalizeCompletion`. Or better, I should check the session phase — if it's already `completed`, don't send another ack.

Actually, the simplest fix: in `maybeFlushPlaybackCompletion`, check the session phase:
```ts
if (!isReadyToAckPlaybackFinished(s)) {
  return;
}
```

The `isReadyToAckPlaybackFinished` selector checks `text.delivered`, `audio.terminal`, `motion.completed`, `backend.turnFinished`. It doesn't check `phase`. So even after `phase = "completed"`, the conditions might still be true.

I should add a check: if phase is already terminal, don't send another ack.

Let me add a check: `if (s.phase === "completed" || s.phase === "failed") return;`

Actually, the cleaner fix: don't reset `completionSent` in `resetPlaybackCoordination` when called from `finalizeCompletion`. OR, just add a `finalized` flag.

Actually, the simplest: check `isReadyToAckPlaybackFinished` and also check that phase is not terminal. Let me add this to `maybeFlushPlaybackCompletion`.

Or even simpler: add a `finalized` flag that prevents any further processing.

Let me just add a terminal phase check to `maybeFlushPlaybackCompletion`:
```ts
if (s.phase === "completed" || s.phase === "failed") {
  return;
}
```

This prevents the double-send without needing extra state.

## Current AstrBot Architecture

### Core Pipeline (original)
The original AstrBot pipeline is:
```
WakingCheck → WhitelistCheck → SessionStatusCheck → RateLimit → ContentSafetyCheck → PreProcess → ProcessStage → ResultDecorate → Respond
```

Platform adapters (`Platform`) register with `PlatformManager`. Events go through the pipeline.

### Interaction Middleware (new layer)
The user added a middleware layer that sits BEFORE the pipeline:

```
Platform Adapter → PlatformManager → CoreInputGateway → InteractionMiddleware → [decision] → Core Queue → Pipeline
```

Key components:
1. `CoreInputGateway` - receives events from platform adapters
2. `InteractionMiddleware` - the main middleware, makes routing decisions (SELF_REPLY, DELEGATE_TO_CORE, HYBRID)
3. `InteractionOutputController` - manages output
4. `InteractionDecisionAgent` - LLM-based decision agent
5. `InteractionMemoryStore` - memory management

### How Plugins Hook In

Currently, the AG99live plugin hooks in via:
1. `@register_platform_adapter("olv_pet_adapter", ...)` on `OLVPetPlatformAdapter` - this registers as a platform adapter
2. `@filter.on_llm_response()` and `@filter.on_decorating_result()` in `main.py` - these are AstrBot filter hooks that fire during the pipeline

But with the middleware, there's a new hook mechanism. The interaction middleware has:
- `InteractionPromptContribution` - plugins can contribute to the prompt
- `InteractionResultContribution` - plugins can contribute to the result
- `InteractionDecisionView` - read-only view of the decision context
- `InteractionStreamView` - read-only view of streaming output
- `InteractionResultView` - read-only view of the result


So `PlatformManager` receives events from platform adapters and passes them to `CoreInputGateway`, which passes them to `InteractionMiddleware.handle_inbound()`.

The middleware then:

2. If not enabled, puts event on `core_queue` (which goes to the pipeline)

For the AG99live plugin, the question is: how should it hook into this new middleware?

So the current architecture has TWO parallel paths:
1. **AG99live Protocol Path**: Frontend → WebSocket → TurnCoordinator → handles AG99live messages directly

The middleware sits BETWEEN the platform adapter and the pipeline. For the AG99live plugin, the OLVPetPlatformAdapter IS the platform adapter. So events from the frontend go through `handle_msg` → `TurnCoordinator` directly.

## Architecture Summary

The AstrBot core now has two parallel systems:

### 1. Original Pipeline (9 stages)
```
WakingCheck → WhitelistCheck → ... → ProcessStage → ResultDecorate → Respond
```
Plugins (Stars) hook via:
- `@register_platform_adapter` — creates Platform instances
- `@filter.on_llm_response()` — fires during pipeline
- `@filter.on_decorating_result()` — fires during pipeline

### 2. Interaction Middleware (new)
```
PlatformAdapter → CoreInputGateway → InteractionMiddleware → [decision] → core_queue → Pipeline
```

The middleware intercepts ALL platform events before they reach the pipeline. It makes LLM-based routing decisions:
- `SELF_REPLY` — handle entirely in middleware
- `DELEGATE_TO_CORE` — forward to pipeline
- `HYBRID` — immediate reply + forward to pipeline

The middleware has:
- `InteractionMemoryStore` — session-level memory
- `InteractionDecisionAgent` — LLM-based decision making
- `InteractionOutputController` — manages output streaming
- Turn state tracking (`turn_state.py`)
- Plugin contributor system (`contributors.py`, `registry.py`)

### How AG99live Currently Works

The plugin registers `OLVPetPlatformAdapter` as a platform adapter. When the frontend sends messages:
1. `handle_msg` → `TurnCoordinator.handle_msg` → processes AG99live protocol
2. For user text, `_commit_inbound_message` creates a platform event → `commit_event`
3. This event goes through `PlatformManager` → `CoreInputGateway` → `InteractionMiddleware`
4. If middleware is NOT enabled for "olv_pet_adapter", goes directly to core_queue → pipeline

The plugin's `@filter.on_llm_response()` and `@filter.on_decorating_result()` hooks fire during the pipeline's ProcessStage.

### What Needs to Change

The user wants the plugin to be "mounted on the middleware". This means:

1. **Enable middleware for "olv_pet_adapter"** — Add config to enable interaction middleware for the AG99live platform
2. **Register AG99live as an interaction contributor** — The plugin should register its capabilities (motion generation, AG99live protocol handling) with the middleware
3. **Handle middleware routing** — The middleware should understand that AG99live has its own TurnCoordinator and shouldn't be intercepted in the same way as chat platforms

The key insight is: the middleware currently handles text-based chat platforms. AG99live is different — it has its own protocol (WebSocket-based, with motion intent, audio, etc.) and its own TurnCoordinator. The middleware needs to:
- Recognize AG99live platform events
- Not intercept the AG99live protocol messages (they should still go to TurnCoordinator)
- But still allow the interaction decision to influence how AstrBot processes the text

Actually, wait. Let me re-think this. The AG99live plugin has TWO paths:
1. **WebSocket protocol messages** (input.text, output.text, control.*, engine.*) — these are handled by TurnCoordinator directly, NOT through the AstrBot pipeline
2. **AstrBot platform events** — when TurnCoordinator creates platform events (via `_commit_inbound_message`), these go through the pipeline

The middleware intercepts path 2. Path 1 is direct WebSocket handling.

So the question is: should the middleware also handle path 1? Or should it only influence path 2?

I think the answer is: the middleware should influence path 2 (the AstrBot pipeline), while path 1 (AG99live protocol) continues to work through TurnCoordinator. The middleware provides:
- Better persona/memory management for the text processing
- Streaming observation
- Turn-level postprocessing

But the AG99live protocol (motion, audio, control) should remain under TurnCoordinator's control.

## AstrBot 交互中间件架构

### 当前两条并行路径

```
Path 1: AG99live 协议（WebSocket 直连）
  前端 → WebSocket → OLVPetPlatformAdapter.handle_msg()
       → TurnCoordinator.handle_msg()  ← 直接处理 AG99live 协议
       → commit_event()                ← 把文本转成 AstrBot 事件

Path 2: AstrBot Pipeline（经过中间件拦截）
  PlatformAdapter → CoreInputGateway → InteractionMiddleware
      ↓ (route_mode 决策)
      ├─ SELF_REPLY    → 中间件自己处理
      ├─ HYBRID        → 先立即回复，再转发 pipeline
      └─ DELEGATE_TO_CORE → core_queue → Pipeline(9 stages)
```

**中间件拦截的是 Path 2**——即 `TurnCoordinator._commit_inbound_message()` 产生的 AstrBot 平台事件。AG99live 的 WebSocket 协议消息（`engine.motion_*`、`control.*`、`output.audio` 等）仍然走 Path 1，不经中间件。

### 中间件能力

| 组件 | 能力 |
|---|---|
| `InteractionDecisionAgent` | LLM 决策：SELF_REPLY / DELEGATE / HYBRID |
| `InteractionMemoryStore` | 会话级交互记忆（独立于 AstrBot 原生 memory） |
| `InteractionOutputController` | 流式输出拦截、utterance 追踪 |
| `turn_state.py` | turn 级状态：utterances、completion、postprocess |
| `contributors.py` | 插件可注册 `InteractionPromptContribution` / `InteractionResultContribution` |
| `collectors.py` | `InteractionMemoryCollector` / `InteractionConversationHistoryCollector`——注入到 LLM prompt context |

### 当前挂载方式 vs 应该的挂载方式

**当前**（`main.py`）：
```python
@filter.on_llm_response()       # Pipeline 钩子——中间件 unaware
@filter.on_decorating_result()  # Pipeline 钩子——中间件 unaware
```

这两个钩子在 Pipeline 的 ProcessStage/ResultDecorate 阶段触发，**完全绕过中间件**。它们做的事情（split_after_reply 动作生成、隐藏 markup 清理）在中间件体系下需要重新定位。

**应该改成**：

1. **`OLVPetPlatformAdapter` 的 `run()` 方法**中——这是平台适配器生命周期入口。中间件已经在 `core_lifecycle.py` 中初始化，`PlatformManager` 持有 `CoreInputGateway`。AG99live 的 platform adapter 需要在 middleware 上注册自己的 **output contributor**，让中间件在 turn finalizer 阶段知道有 AG99live 的 motion 生成逻辑需要执行。

2. **`@filter.on_llm_response()` → `InteractionResultContribution`**：`split_after_reply` 的动作生成逻辑应该注册为中间件的 result contributor，在 `HYBRID` 或 `DELEGATE_TO_CORE` 模式下的 turn 完成时被调用。

3. **`@filter.on_decorating_result()` → `InteractionOutputController` 集成**：隐藏 markup 清理应该在中间件的 output controller 中完成（`capture_message_chain` / `capture_streaming` 阶段），而不是等 pipeline 的 ResultDecorate 阶段。

4. **中间件配置**：需要在 AstrBot 配置中为 `olv_pet_adapter` 启用 interaction middleware：
   ```yaml
   interaction_middleware:
     enabled: true
     platforms:
       olv_pet_adapter:
         enabled: true
   ```

---

### 建议的下一步

| 优先级 | 任务 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 启用中间件对 `olv_pet_adapter` 的配置 | 最小改动，让中间件开始拦截 AG99live 的平台事件 |
| 2 | 将 `@filter.on_llm_response` 逻辑迁移为 `InteractionResultContribution` | 让 split_after_reply 动作生成挂到中间件的 turn finalizer |
| 3 | 将 `@filter.on_decorating_result` 逻辑集成到 output controller | markup 清理在流式输出阶段完成 |
| 4 | 移除旧的 pipeline filter 钩子 | 清理 `main.py` 中的旧代码 |

## 配置侧（AstrBot 本体，你来改）

`data/cmd_config.json` 中只需改一行——把 `olv_pet_adapter` 加到 `default_enabled_for_platforms`：

```json
"interaction_middleware": {
    "enabled": true,
    "default_enabled_for_platforms": [
      "webchat",
      "olv_pet_adapter"    // ← 加这一行
    ],
    ...
}
```

这样 `CoreInputGateway → InteractionMiddleware.handle_inbound()` 就会拦截 AG99live 的平台事件。

---

## 插件侧（AG99live，需要做的）

**当前 `main.py` 的两个 `@filter` 钩子在 middleware 启用后仍然有效**——只要 middleware 决策是 `DELEGATE_TO_CORE`，事件就会进入 pipeline，filter 钩子照常触发。两条路径自然兼容：

```
Path 1 (middleware 关):   PlatformAdapter → Pipeline → @filter 钩子
Path 2 (middleware 开):   PlatformAdapter → Middleware → DELEGATE_TO_CORE → Pipeline → @filter 钩子
```

---

## ⚠️ 需要注意的风险

Middleware 的 LLM 决策可能给 AG99live 事件返回 `SELF_REPLY`，这时 **pipeline 被完全绕过**，TurnCoordinator 的 `emit_message_chain` 不执行——前端收不到完整的文本+音频+动作回复。

当前 middleware 有 `_maybe_bypass_protocol_command`（检测 `/` 等唤醒前缀），但不保证 LLM 决策不会对普通文本返回 `SELF_REPLY`。

**如果要彻底防止**，需要在 middleware 里加一个 `force_delegate` 平台级配置——改动很小（`_handle_inbound_async` 里 4 行），但那是 AstrBot 核心代码。你也可以选择先不加，观察实际决策行为——因为 middleware 的 LLM 对"需要执行能力"的消息默认走 `DELEGATE_TO_CORE`，AG99live 的用户消息几乎都属于这类，SELF_REPLY 触发概率应该很低。

---

## Interaction Middleware 回复逻辑全景

### 三条路由

```
用户消息 → Middleware.handle_inbound()
              │
              ├─ LLM 决策 (_decide_or_fallback)
              │
              ├─ SELF_REPLY      中间件自己回复，不经过 pipeline
              │   ├─ emit_immediate_reply()    发送立即回复文本
              │   ├─ complete_visible_turn()   标记本轮可见完成
              │   └─ _finalize_turn()          持久化记忆 + postprocess
              │
              ├─ HYBRID          立即回复 + 委托 pipeline
              │   ├─ emit_immediate_reply()
              │   └─ _forward_to_core()  → pipeline → LLM → Respond
              │
              └─ DELEGATE_TO_CORE  全权委托 pipeline
                  └─ _forward_to_core()  → pipeline → LLM → Respond
```

### 输出拦截（`_install_core_output_interceptor`）

无论走 HYBRID 还是 DELEGATE_TO_CORE，middleware 都会 monkey-patch `event` 的三个方法：

```
event.send()              → 被 Middleware 拦截 → InteractionOutputController.capture_message_chain()
event.send_streaming()    → 被 Middleware 拦截 → InteractionOutputController.capture_streaming()
event.complete_visible_turn() → 被 Middleware 拦截 → InteractionOutputController.capture_visible_completion()
```

**这意味着**：pipeline 里 LLM 产出的所有文字，都经过 middleware 的 output controller——用于 utterance 追踪、流式观察、交互记忆。

### 当前 AG99live 的问题

AG99live 的 TurnCoordinator 有自己独立的输出路径：

```
LLM 响应 → @filter.on_llm_response()
         → TurnCoordinator.emit_message_chain()
         → send_json()  ← WebSocket 直发，完全绕过 Middleware
```

Middleware 拦截的是 `event.send()`（pipeline 输出），但 TurnCoordinator 用的是 `send_json()`（WebSocket 直发）。**两条输出路径并行存在，中间件看不到 TurnCoordinator 的输出。**

---

## 你说的"挂载到中间件"是对的

正确的架构应该是：

```
用户消息 → Middleware → DELEGATE_TO_CORE → Pipeline → LLM
                                                    ↓
                                    event.send() → [中间件拦截]
                                                    ↓
                                    InteractionOutputController
                                                    ↓
                                    event.complete_visible_turn()
                                                    ↓
                                    _finalize_turn()
                                        ├─ memory persist
                                        ├─ postprocess dispatch
                                        └─ ← 这里应该挂 AG99live 的贡献
```

AG99live 插件不应该作为 pipeline 的 `@filter` 钩子运行，而应该注册为中间件的 **contributor**，在中间件的 `_finalize_turn` 阶段被调用。

具体来说：

| 旧位置 | 新位置 | 机制 |
|---|---|---|
| `@filter.on_llm_response()` → `schedule_motion_after_reply` | `InteractionResultContribution` | 中间件 finalize 时调用 |
| `@filter.on_decorating_result()` → `sanitize_hidden_output_markup` | `InteractionOutputController.capture_message_chain` | 输出阶段直接过滤 |
| TurnCoordinator 的协议输出 | 中间件的 `_finalize_turn` postprocess | 由 contributor 接管 |

这样做的好处：
1. **单一输出路径**——不再有 pipeline `event.send()` 和 TurnCoordinator `send_json()` 两条路
2. **中间件可见**——AG99live 的输出（文本、音频、动作）都在中间件的 turn 生命周期内
3. **两条路径自动兼容**——middleware 关时用旧 `@filter` 钩子，开时走 contributor

## 需要做的事情

### 第一层：AstrBot 配置（你改）

`AstrBot/data/cmd_config.json` 加一行：

```json
"interaction_middleware": {
    "default_enabled_for_platforms": [
      "webchat",
      "olv_pet_adapter"    // ← 加这一行
    ]
}
```

### 第二层：AG99live 插件结构（我来写，但需要你确认方向）

目前 `main.py` 里两个 `@filter` 钩子直接从 pipeline 拦截 LLM 输出。中间件启用后，LLM 输出先经过 middleware 的 output controller，再进 pipeline。**旧 `@filter` 钩子在中介件下仍能触发**（因为 `DELEGATE_TO_CORE` 仍走 pipeline），但长期来看两个路径并行会有分歧。

我建议的方向是分两步走：

**第一步（最小改动，先让中间件拦截生效）**：不改插件代码，只改配置。验证中间件拦截 AG99live 事件后 pipeline 仍正常运转，`@filter` 钩子仍触发。两条路径此时自然兼容。

**第二步（把插件真正挂到中间件上）**：需要动 `services/frontend_compat_service.py` 和 `main.py`。从 `@filter` 钩子迁移到中间件的 contributor 机制。这涉及在中间件初始化时注册 AG99live 的 `InteractionResultContribution`，让它在 turn finalize 阶段接管 motion 生成和协议输出。

6. If SELF_REPLY: middleware handles reply, pipeline NOT called, @filter NOT fired

So the plugin should work identically regardless of whether middleware is present or not. The middleware should be transparent to the plugin.

The simplest way to achieve this: ensure middleware always routes AG99live events to DELEGATE_TO_CORE. Then the pipeline runs normally, @filter hooks fire normally, TurnCoordinator works normally. The middleware just adds memory/persona enrichment but doesn't change the flow.

The user needs to add `force_delegate: true` for olv_pet_adapter in the middleware config, AND the middleware needs to support this (which it currently doesn't).

OR - the user could configure the middleware's LLM decision to always route olv_pet_adapter to delegate. But that requires changing the decision logic.

```python
            completed = await self._complete_visible_turn_or_record_failure(event)
            if completed:

            return
        if decision.route_mode == RouteMode.HYBRID:
            if decision.should_emit_immediate_reply:
                await self._emit_immediate_reply_or_record_failure(event, decision)
            self._forward_to_core(event)
            return
        if decision.should_emit_immediate_reply:
            await self._emit_immediate_reply_or_record_failure(event, decision)
        self._forward_to_core(event)
```

Now, there's another question: does the middleware's `_install_core_output_interceptor` interfere with AG99live's TurnCoordinator output?

The interceptor replaces `event.send`, `event.send_streaming`, and `event.complete_visible_turn` on the AstrMessageEvent object. These are called during the pipeline's Respond stage.

For AG99live:
- The pipeline runs normally (when DELEGATE_TO_CORE)
- The LLM generates a response
- The pipeline calls `event.send()` - this is intercepted by middleware
- The interceptor captures the message chain in `InteractionOutputController`
- The original `send` is still called eventually

Then, separately:
- `@filter.on_llm_response()` fires
- TurnCoordinator.emit_message_chain() sends via `send_json()` (WebSocket)

The interceptor doesn't affect TurnCoordinator because TurnCoordinator uses `send_json` not `event.send`.

So the current architecture with middleware enabled (DELEGATE_TO_CORE) would work like this:
1. Middleware intercepts event, decides DELEGATE_TO_CORE
2. Event goes to pipeline
3. LLM generates response → pipeline calls `event.send()` → middleware captures for memory → original send also happens
4. @filter.on_llm_response() fires → TurnCoordinator emits via WebSocket
5. Both outputs happen: pipeline's `event.send()` AND TurnCoordinator's WebSocket output

## 结论：插件代码不需要改

当前 `main.py` 的两个 `@filter` 钩子在中间件启用后**仍然有效**。原因是：

```
Middleware (DELEGATE_TO_CORE) → Pipeline (正常运转) → @filter 钩子照常触发
```

中间件的 fallback 决策就是 `DELEGATE_TO_CORE`，AG99live 的用户消息对于 LLM 决策者来说看起来是需要"核心执行"的，所以大概率不会被路由到 `SELF_REPLY`。

**两条路径自然兼容**：

| 场景 | 数据流 | `@filter` 触发？ |
|---|---|---|
| 中间件关（原版 AstrBot） | Platform → Pipeline | ✅ |
| 中间件开，决策 DELEGATE_TO_CORE | Platform → Middleware → Pipeline | ✅ |
| 中间件开，决策 HYBRID | Platform → Middleware（先发一句）→ Pipeline | ✅ |
| 中间件开，决策 SELF_REPLY | Platform → Middleware（自己回） | ❌ 但概率极低 |

---

## 唯一风险

LLM 决策者偶尔可能对 AG99live 的简单寒暄返回 `SELF_REPLY`。此时 pipeline 被绕过，TurnCoordinator 不执行——前端只收到一行纯文本。

**防止方式**（如果你觉得需要）：在中间件里加一个 `force_delegate` 平台选项，4 行代码。或者在 `cmd_config.json` 的 `platforms.olv_pet_adapter` 里加 `"force_delegate": true`，然后 middleware 读这个标志。

但可以先不加——观察实际运行，SELF_REPLY 在实际 AG99live 场景下触发概率应该极低。

---
